公司簡介 
華夏成立於 1964 年 ， 屬於台聚集團成員之
一，為國內 PVC 第二大生產商，旗下轉投
資子公司 VCM 廠台氯（持股 87%） 。華夏
2025 營 收 結 構 包 括 PVC /VCM 產品  
66%、PVC 製品（膠布、建材、膠皮等）
24%、鹼氯產品 10%，外銷區域以亞洲區
域為主 ， 包括印度 、 孟加拉 、 東南亞 、 中東 、
中國等地區。華夏原料部分，VCM 由台氯
供應，EDC 主要由台塑及國外廠商供應，
乙烯則是由中油和國外廠商供應。

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手：

葉家宏 bill.yeh@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

華夏 (1305 TT) 
PVC 供給壓力仍大，壓抑利差表現

永豐觀點 
原料乙烯高檔回落，中國 PVC 社會庫存壓力仍大，造成 PVC 市場行情反轉，利差
表現受限，故研究部維持保守看待華夏獲利表現 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持中立：中國 PVC 社會庫存壓力大，而反內卷政策仍未見顯著成效，
造成 PVC 供給壓力延續，華夏將進行產銷調節，降低庫存，優先以國內市場銷售
為主，並避開紅海市場競爭，尋求獲利機會，研究部考量原料乙烯報價仍高，將壓
抑 PVC 利差表現，故研究部預期華夏營運表現將持續承壓，預估 2026 EPS -1.27
元，維持中立投資建議。

ESG 評析 
華夏企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

華夏為國內 PVC 第二大生產商：華夏成立於 1964 年，屬於台聚集團成員之一，
為國內 PVC 第二大生產商，旗下轉投資子公司 VCM 廠台氯（持股 87%） 。華夏
2025 營收結構包括 PVC /VCM 產品 66%、PVC 製品（膠布 、 建材 、 膠皮等）24%、
鹼氯產品 10%，外銷區域以亞洲區域為主，包括印度、孟加拉、東南亞、中東、中
國等地區。華夏原料部分，VCM 由台氯供應，EDC 主要由台塑及國外廠商供應，
乙烯則是由中油和國外廠商供應。 
1Q26 稅後 EPS 為-0.21 元，略低於預期：華夏 1Q26 營收 25.11 億元（+45.7% 
QoQ，+8.6% YoY） ，優於預期，主因美伊戰爭推升PVC 報價，且下游客戶擔心斷
料風險而積極購料備貨，帶動營收表現，毛利率 1.97%（+1.32 pts. QoQ，+6.26 
pts. YoY） ，低於預期，主因華夏低價原料庫存多用於4M26 生產，1Q26 所使用
之低價原料庫存比例低於預期，稅後虧損 1.2 億元（虧損縮小 QoQ，虧損縮小 
YoY），1Q26 稅後 EPS -0.21 元，略低於預期。 
2Q26 虧損幅度預期將收斂：展望 2Q26，中國製造業在五一長假後，開工復甦緩
慢，為去化過高的電石法 PVC 社會庫存，短期增加外銷低價傾銷亞洲市場，而因
美伊戰事趨於和緩，下游廠商購買力道也開始出現縮手，造成 PVC 報價於 5M26
開始出現行情反轉 。 研究部評估受新三輕設備事故影響 ， 華夏2Q26 稼動率將受影
響，因 PVC 報價預期較 1Q26 季增，將推升 2Q26 營收表現，且公司具備低價庫
存效應 ， 獲利表現亦有望得到改善 ， 基此 ， 研究部預估2Q26 營收 33.23 億元 （+24.3
％QoQ，+25.0％ YoY） ，毛利率8.82%（+6.85 pts. QoQ，+16.05 pts. YoY），
稅後虧損 0.2 億元（虧損縮小 QoQ，虧損縮小 YoY） ，稅後EPS -0.04 元。

PVC 供給壓力仍大，維持中立投資建議：展望後市，受到中國貨源低價傾銷亞洲
市場，及原料乙烯走勢疲軟影響，PVC 報價趨於下跌，而受制於原料乙烯報價位居
高檔影響，PVC 利差將面臨下行壓力，而中國反內卷政策仍未見顯著成效，造成
PVC 供給壓力延續，華夏將進行產銷調節，降低庫存，優先以國內市場銷售為主，
並避開紅海市場競爭，開發供需結構較為健康的海外市場，尋求獲利機會。研究部
考量目前中國 PVC 社會庫存水位仍位於近年同期新高水準 ， 中國電石法PVC 稼動

What’s Changed
WT Microelectronics Co. Ltd.
(3036.TW) From To
Price Target NT$299.00 NT$349.00
WPG Holdings (3702.TW) From To
Price Target NT$121.00 NT$160.00
WPG reported strong 1Q earnings:    WPG reported its 1Q results. The 1Q revenue
was NT$316.5bn, +24%     Q/Q, +27% Y/Y, in line with MSe. Revenue was driven by AI
infra     build-out and semiconductors demand, and expanded supply chain     services

(e.g., LaaS). 1Q gross profit was NT$14.2bn, gross margin was 4.5%, +0.2ppt Q/Q and
+0.8ppt Y/Y, 0.2ppt above our forecast. 1Q operating profit was NT$8.5bn, with
operating margin at 2.7%, +0.5ppt Q/Q and  +1.0ppt Y/Y, 0.2ppt above our estimate.
1Q (diluted)     EPS was NT$3.17, +95% Q/Q and +192% Y/Y, 12% above our estimate.
ROWC reached     15.2%, up from 11.5% in 4Q25. This follows the strong 1Q results of
WT Micro: Scale drives operating leverage

.
Strong 2Q guidance by WPG: The company guided for mid-point 2Q revenue of NT
$355bn, gross margin of     4.35%, operating margin of 2.58% and (non-diluted) EPS of
NT$3.86. The strong     outlook is driven by continued global AI demand and capex
guidance,     and management expects continued strong demand from inference and
edge AI.
Other highlights from WPG 1Q earnings
: By application type, compute,
communication, consumer, industrial and auto     accounted for 49%, 15%, 8%, 7% and

7%, respectively, which shows strong growth for     compute and moderate growth for
industrial and auto. By device type,     memory and core component accounted for 45%
and 23%, respectively. The reduction in     the core component mix from 31% in 4Q25
represents the company's     drive to improve margins.
The AI story lifts earnings for both WT and WPG:
Based on the actual 1Q results
for WPG and strong guidance for 2Q     forward, we raise our earnings estimates:

•  WT – EPS up 12%,  17% and 16% for 2026-28, respectively;
• WPG – EPS up 32% for each 2026-28.
We raise price targets for  WT from NT$299 to NT$349 and for      WPG from NT
$121 to NT$160:    We continue to use residual income valuation models; PT changes
reflect our earnings estimate revisions.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
WPG reported strong 1Q earnings with EPS 12% above MSe.
WPG 2Q mid-point guidance implies 12% Q/Q revenue growth and 17% Q/Q EPS
growth.
WPG management remains positive on the secular AI-driven demand.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 060: 260521_daiwa_TSMT

Source PDF: all report pdf/260521_daiwa_TSMT.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-21
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: We met with Taiwan Surface Mounting Technology (TSMT)’s 
management on 21 May (see our 13 May flash). Per management, its 
product mix for 2025 was: TFT-LCD 46%, Mini LED 11%, DRAM module 
16%, auto 9%, cable 9%, touch 5%, and others 4%. Management guides 
for the mix for 2026 to be: TFT-LCD 46%, Mini LED 11%, DRAM module 
17%, auto 8%, cable 6%, touch 5%, and others 7%. The QFII holding of 
TSMT was 18.2% on 20 May vs 19.8% at the end of 2025.

Highlights: 2026-27 guidance. TSMT guides for YoY revenue growth of 5-
10% (close to the high end; +1.9% YoY revenue growth for 4M26) for 2026 
and a double-digit % YoY growth for 2027, thanks to the multiple revenue 
drivers from AI-related demand. For 2026, the company expects gross 
margin of 13-15% (up QoQ; vs 12.8% gross margin in 2025), with further 
YoY growth in 2027 on a more favourable product mix. Its AI-related 
revenue target is 15-20% for 2027, up from 5-6% in 4Q26 and 1-2% in 
2026. For 2026, the company expects 2H26 revenue to be higher than that 
in 1H26, thanks to the rising order outlook from DRAM modules and AI-
related revenue supported by its capacity ramping up from its Mexico, 
India, and Vietnam plants.

Multiple revenue drivers to kick off from 2H26. TSMT mentioned several 
revenue drivers as follows: 1) Optical transceiver assembly. TSMT will be 
the major supplier of 800G and 1.6T products to its US-based client, and 
the company has seen some outsourcing demand from China-based 
clients, with 4-5% of combined revenue contribution in 4Q26. 2) DRAM 
module. TSMT expects the revenue contribution to rise to 20-25% in 2H26, 
up from 18% in 2Q26, and to grow YoY in 2027 considering its US- and 
China-based clients. 3) Low earth orbit (LEO). TSMT will start delivering 
SMT products in 2H26 for a US-based client. 4) Humanoid robots. The 
company sees business opportunity (sensor modules and PCBA) from the 
US-based client with revenue contribution from 2027, likely to reach a low 
single-digit % of 2027 revenue. 5) Battery backup unit (BBU). The 
company will trial run products in 4Q26 or 2027. 6) Server SMT. Currently, 
TSMT has two clients and plans to add a new one in 2H26 from Taiwan or 
the US. Thus, the company guides for capex of USD100-150m over 2026-
27, up from USD100m in 2025, subject to its capacity expansion plan in 
Mexico, as it expects capacity constraints for SMT in the next 1-2 years 
due to the increasing use of AI applications. The company also plans to 
expand its production capacity in China, Vietnam, Taiwan, and Mexico.

Valuation: The stock is currently trading at PERs of 21.4x and 17.4x based 
on the Bloomberg consensus’ 2026E and 2027E EPS, respectively.

21 May 2026

Positive tone from management

➢ TSMT expects stronger YoY revenue growth for 2027 vs 2026 
➢ Several new drivers to underpin revenue outlook over 2026-27 
➢ AI-related revenue to reach 15-20% of 2027 revenue vs 1-2% for 2026

Idea
AP Memory Technology Corp  | Asia Pacific
Too early to be concerned
about Si-Cap competition; OW
We think the market is overly concerned oabout competition in
the early cycle. Our supply/demand model suggests ongoing
supply shortage in the next three years. Time to accumulate
more.
Our further thoughts on the Si-Cap competition  (link): SEMCO (covered by
Shawn Kim) was already a player in the Si-Cap market, but we believe its recently
announced ~US$1bn contract introduces competition to AP Memory's market share.

We believe SEMCO's Si-Cap business uses a stacking technology as well and aims to
address a similar application –  i.e., EMIB; the advantage is the better foundry process
from a potential Taiwanese partner, Winbond. That said, we think it will take time to
acquire the equipment and go through qualification with substrate vendors, EMIB,
and end customers.
Our supply/demand model suggests supply shortage in the next three years: For
the non-TSM silicon capacitor market, we expect supply shortage of 57%for 2026,

16% for 2027, and 11% for 2028 (please refer to our supply/demand model: Exhibit
1 ). We see EMIB (TPU v10/v11 and potentially auto chips in the future), AI GPUs in
China, and smartphone SoC as major market opportunities for silicon capacitors.
Supply remains constrained with limited global capacity available.
Remain OW on AP Memory; VHM is the next driver: We maintain our earnings
estimates and price target at NT$1,555. In addition to Si-Cap, we think the market

overlooks another big opportunity from VHM.
Remain OW on Powerchip
: Powerchip is fully loaded with pricing up on all products
(analog, logic, memory).
Remain EW on Winbond:
We believe SEMCO will act as a fabless design company
for its new contract, leveraging Winbond as its foundry partner. We view this as
positive for Winbond, but we expect limited revenue contribution –   only a low-
single-digit pat of the  revenue mix in 2027e. Our price target is unchanged.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Winbond Electronics Corp (2344.TW)
Base case, 2.7x 2026e P/B. This method is in line with the approach we adopt for Greater China
memory IDM peers, in view of the industry's high volatility. We believe the stock merits a mul-
tiple beyond the high end of its historical band of 0.5-1.5x since 2017, as we are seeing DRAM
pricing upside (even though pricing power could moderate into 2H26) and a sustainable NOR

price hike.
Risks to Upside
n NOR Flash pricing increase, driven by stronger-than-expected demand
n Stronger-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing supply shortage
n Faster-than-expected SLC NAND development
Risks to Downside
n NOR Flash downcycle, driven by weaker demand
n Lower-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing oversupply
n Slower-than-expected SLC NAND development
AP Memory Technology Corp (6531.TW)
Base case, derived from a residual income model. Key RI model assumptions include 1) cost

of equity constant at 9.2% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.2 beta); 2) payout ratio of
67%; 3) terminal growth rate of 3.0%; and 4) medium-term growth rate of 16.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected consumer demand
n Faster-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development
n Milder competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other
design houses
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected consumer demand
n Slower-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development

n Intensifying competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other
design houses
Powerchip Semiconductor Manufacturing Co (6770.TW)
Base case, 2.1x 2026e P/B. This method is in line with our approach for Greater China memory
IDM players, in view of the industry's high volatility. Our 2.1x target P/B is higher than the
historical average of 1.5x since 2021, reflecting our optimistic view on improved pricing and
utilization.
Risks to Upside

n Pricing power is sustained in 2026.
n Listing on main board attracts investment fund flows.

n Aluminum process enjoys higher margins.
Risks to Downside
n Competition from China intensifies.
n Price erosion is faster than expected.
n Demand from consumer electronics clients weakens further.
n Inventory in the supply chain takes longer to digest.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Update
WinWay Technology Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
No Changes in Fundamentals
With Continual Ramp Up in
Socket Capacity; OW
What's new? Winway shares fell 8.5% today vs. the Taiex up 2.2%, mainly on
concerns about market share loss in Vera CPU probe cards. Investors also worry
about the capacity ramp up given weak April sales (-19% M/M).
Our view: Our checks suggest no major changes in Winway’s Vera CPU market share
vs. MPI, with the ramp up already underway in 1Q. We still see no change to 2Q

revenue, which we expect to grow 15-20% Q/Q, though likely more June-loaded. We
view this pullback as a good entry point to accumulate a stock that enjoys longer
testing time and larger package size upgrades. The company will host its earnings
call next Tuesday, where we expect discussion of the future capacity plan and a CPO
revenue update.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

Valuation Methodology and Risks
WinWay Technology Co Ltd (6515.TW)
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture
long-term value. We assume a 9.14% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium
5.5%), a payout ratio of 80%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.4%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards

n Faster-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n Stronger-than-expected edge AI demand
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards
n Slower-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n No edge AI demand in the next few years

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 068: 260525_daiwa_Ta Liang

Source PDF: all report pdf/260525_daiwa_Ta Liang.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-25
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: Ta Liang Technology is a PCB routing and drilling machine 
maker serving PCB manufacturers, with high-end Charge-Coupled Devices 
(CCDs) commanding more than 2x the ASP of regular machines. The 
company operates production facilities in Taoyuan, Lianshui, and Nanjing, 
with a combined monthly capacity of c.300 units. CCDs are primarily 
adopted for back-drilling, though through-hole precision demand is 
increasingly emerging as an incremental growth driver. Ta Liang is also 
launching a patent-protected PCB inner layer thickness metrology (TM) 
machine to complement CCDs at an ideal TM:CCD ratio of 1:8, while 
additionally serving the semiconductor industry with metrology, AOI, and 
automation solutions.

Highlights: Ta Liang and Schmoll (not listed, Germany) are the only 
machine makers globally owning in-house controllers, creating a strong 
technology moat in the CCD market. Ta Liang continues to gain share from 
Schmoll through competitive pricing, solid brand recognition among PCB 
makers, and favourable domestic policy tailwinds supporting China-made 
equipment. In standard drilling and routing, Chinese competitors Vega CNC 
(not listed) and Hans Laser (002008 CH, NR) hold significantly larger 
capacity, though the competitive landscape in this segment has remained 
relatively stable. The patent-protected TM machine, which bundles 
naturally with CCDs, provides an additional lever to deepen customer 
relationships and reinforce share gains in the high-end segment.

Looking ahead, we see multiple growth catalysts supporting Ta Liang's 
growth trajectory. Demand for CCDs is expanding beyond back-drilling into 
through-hole processes where precision requirements are increasing, while 
the TM machine serves as a natural bundle driver to further accelerate 
CCD adoption and deepen wallet share per customer. CCDs carry high 
ASPs and deliver more than double the margins of regular machines, 
driving a favourable product mix shift alongside steady growth in regular 
machine demand underpinned by PCB makers’ expanding capex cycle. 
Reflecting strong commercial momentum, Ta Liang has secured orders at 
full capacity through 2026, with near-term upside from outsourced capacity 
additions of c.50 incremental units for regular machines and a potential 
expansion from the current single working shift to a maximum of three 
shifts in Nan Jing Plant.

Valuation: The stock is trading at 108x PER and 23x PBR for 2025 (vs. its 
PBR range of 1-10x prior to 2026), reflecting the market’s recognition of Ta 
Liang’s high-end product transition and earnings upside potential from 
CCDs and TM machine.

25 May 2026

High-precision moat in PCB machines

➢ Sole Chinese PCB machine maker with an in-house CCD controller 
➢ Capacity fully booked through 2026 
➢ TM machine bundles drive deeper wallet share from customers

"2": the security is expected to outperform the local index by 5-15% over the next 12 months. 
"3": the security is expected to perform within 5% of the local index (better or worse) over the next 12 months. 
"4": the security is expected to underperform the local index by 5-15% over the next 12 months. 
"5": the security could underperform the local index by more than 15% over the next 12 months.

創意 (3443 TT)  Buy

未來美系客戶貢獻力道增加中，調升至 Buy。  
投資建議 ：創意 2026 年 Turnkey 營收預計成長幅度和 2025 年
相近約 YoY+55~60% 。NRE 業務方面 ， 由於專案難度提升 ，4Q25
開始進行  AI 推論晶片專案，開發重點包含車用  IC 與光通訊
相關產品，但 2026 年  NRE 營收受成本與開發難度增加影響，
可能呈現下滑趨勢。毛利率方面，因  Turnkey 佔比提升，毛利
率將承壓，惟公司預期毛利率不低於  4Q25 水準。營業費用方
面，公司將持續維持高額研發費用， 2026 年圖靈中心將開始運
作，因此預估營業費用 YoY+15% 以上。預估創意 2026 年稅後
EPS 40.17 元。創意受惠主要美系 CSP 客戶訂單持續增加中，
2026 產能安排與交付較年初改善，將有利創意營收和獲利進一
步成長，調升至 Buy ，目標價至 6,040 元 (2027PER65X) 。  
1Q26 稅後 EPS 12.29 元：創意公告 1Q26 營收 114.48 億元，
QoQ-7.68% ， 稅後淨利 16.46 億元 ，QoQ+41.96% ，稅 後EPS 12.29
元。 NRE&IP 和 Turnkey 各佔營收 14% 和 86% 。 1Q26 毛利率優
於預期，毛利率 27.20% 較 4Q25 增加 8.15 個百分點，主要因
NRE 營收認列多屬於設計工程段的認列，且為車用相關，因此
毛利率較佳。同時 Turnkey 營收毛利率亦較 4Q25 提升，因為
4Q25 認列較多毛利率相對低的比特幣案， 1Q26 Turnkey 的案子
除 CSP 大廠的 CPU 外，還有 BMC 等毛利率較佳的案子。  
ASIC 訂單動能持續 ：創意股東會總經理戴尚義表示， 2025 年
底 Google 研發的 Gemini 3 發布後，多項基準測試超越競爭對
手，其核心關鍵在於 Google 自研晶片具備能效比與成本紅利。
Google 開發者大會  (I/O 2026) 正式宣布進入代理式
Gemini(Agentic Gemini) 。 Google 強調軟硬體「全棧式（ Full 
Stack ）」優化，不僅使用自家  TPU 驅動模型，也會結合自研
的  Google Axion CPU 等硬體來支撐龐大的雲端工作負載與程
式碼執行。受惠此浪潮創意提供客戶 CoWoS 與 Physical Design
的專業支援，協助客戶開發 AI 加速晶片，預期在 N2 及 N3 等
先進製程專案將維持健康動能。

  創意電子  ESG 政策：  
創意自成立以來，除專注在 IC 設計服務本業中追求最大成就外，同時對於
客戶、股東、政府機關、社會、供應商及本公司員工等利害關係人建立良好
的互動及平等對待，並積極落實企業社會公民責任，以實現永續經營之理
念。  
公司執行方針  
誠信正直方面，創意提倡誠信經營及道德從業行為，並落實平衡各利害關係
人的利益，驅動產業的正向循環，共同成為社會向上提升的力量。  
強化環保方面，創意致力於環境永續，堅持綠色設計與綠色供應鏈，追求資
源最佳效率，以達成減廢與污染防治的目標。  
社會關懷方面，創意結合公司資源與同仁自發性的愛心及志工服務，以金
錢、物資及人力等方式投入『環境保育』及『偏鄉、弱勢對象』為兩大範疇，
期能推動永續環境、並能提供弱勢團體必要的生活協助及急難救助，以建立
良好互助社會。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

1 
5285 界霖 (Jih Lin)

報告日期： 2026/05/25 研 究 員：廖翊辰 (Mike Liao) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一.  AI 與車用產品帶動營運回升 ， 4 月營收創歷史新高：   
界霖 1Q26 營收 13.6 億元，QoQ+7.6%，YoY+1.6%，主要受惠
於車用導線架 、 AI 伺服器電源散熱產品及工控需求同步回升 ，  
4Q25 開始多項新產品陸續放量出貨，加上稼動率提升、產品
組合改善，以及銅價上漲後逐步反映售價，使 1Q26 營收與獲
利同步成長，4 月營收 5.8 億元創新高，營收已重新站回單月
5 億元以上水準，未來在車用與 AI 產品持續成長下，預期後
續營運動能可望延續，1Q26 毛利率 18.7%大幅提升，主因高
毛利 AI 相關產品比重增加 ， 產品組合優化使獲利能力顯著改
善，營業利益 1.3 億元，QoQ+100.7%，YoY+153.2%，稅後淨
利 1.02 億元 QoQ+90.1%，YoY+133.3%，EPS 1.0 元。

二.  車用與 AI 電源散熱產品，將成未來主要成長動能：  
界霖目前產品聚焦於封裝材料與功率導線架，主要應用在車
用、工控、AI 伺服器、Edge AI 等領域，公司表示，近年已
將原本應用於車用逆變器的雙面冷卻散熱技術，逐步延伸至
AI 電源散熱領域，並開發頂部散熱與 Clip 等產品，成功切入
AI 伺服器電源供應鏈 。 AI 資料中心未來最核心的問題之一將
是電力與散熱 ， 而功率元件的重要性將持續提升 ， 另一方面，
車用功率元件需求仍維持長期成長趨勢，傳統燃油車平均半
導體單車使用價值約 750 美元，但電動車到 2025 年將提升至
1400 美元，2030 年進一步提升至 1600 美元，高 階車款甚至
達 2500 美元，隨著全球電動車出貨持續成長，有利界霖長期
成長。

三.預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 4.39 元： 
展望 2026 年，界霖積極布局高速成長的 AI 市場，隨著全球
AI 應用持續擴大，AI 資料中心對電源架構持續升級，功率半
導體與高階散熱需求將同步成長，封測市場部分，2024~2030
年中國封裝測試市場規模將由約 300 億美元成長至 500 億美
元，CAGR 約 7.5%，中國仍為全球最大封測市場，而東協地
區 CAGR 9.9%，主要受惠於國際半導體供應鏈重組與東南亞
產能擴張。界霖目前已布局台灣、中國、日本與馬來西亞，
可同步受惠於全球封測與功率半導體需求成長。 預估 2026 年
營收 60.4 億元，YoY+12.9%，毛利率 18.06%，營業利益 5.6
投資評等：買進

四.投資建議買進，目標價 101 元： 
隨著 AI 伺服器電源散熱、車用功率元件、高階導線架以及先
進封裝與封測需求持續增加，界霖可望受惠於 AI、半導體封
測與電動車長期成長趨勢，未來產品組合改善，將帶動獲利
能力持續提升，依 2026 年預估 EPS 計算，目前 P/E 約 18.6
倍，建議買進，目標價 101 元(23 x2026EPS)。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況 
一. 公司簡介 
界霖成立於 2000 年，主要從事半導體導線架、封裝材料與功率元
件相關產品製造，2017 年公司與 SMM（Sumitomo Metal Mining）
合作，收購其海外事業體，取得日本高階導線架技術與海外據點，
並建立台灣、日本、中國與馬來西亞的全球布局，公司客戶涵蓋
國際功率 IDM 大廠與封裝代工廠 （OSAT），包 括 I n f i n e o n、O N s e m i、
STMicroelectronics、Renesas、ROHM、三菱電機、Amkor、強茂等。

二. 營運概況及展望

半導體封測產業： 
全球封裝測試市場持續成長 ， 中國 2024 年市場規模約 300 億美元 ，
預估至 2030 年將提升至約 500 億美元，年複合成長率( CAGR )約 
7.5%，東協市場成長潛力最大 CAGR 9.9%，台灣 CAGR 5.1%，界
霖因具備台灣、中國、日本與馬來西亞多地布局，可直接受惠於
全球封測產能轉移與區域化趨勢。

車用半導體產業： 
車用半導體主要受惠於電動車滲透率提升、車輛電動化與功率半
導體含量增加。根據英飛凌數據指出，傳統燃油車平均半導體元
件單車價值約 750 美元，而純電動車 2025 年已接近 1,400 美
元，2030 年高階車款更可達 1,600 美元，顯示 單車半導體單價及
用量持續提升，數量方面也是持續成長，電動車銷量預計將從去
年約 1400 萬輛成長至 2600 萬輛，此外自駕與智慧座艙發展，
也將進一步推升功率元件需求。

AI 伺服器與資料中心產業： 
AI 伺服器為界霖近年新成長動能，隨著 AI 算力快速提升，市場
焦點已從 GPU 延伸至電力供應與散熱管理，公司切入 AI 電源
管理與高散熱需求產品 ， 包括雙面冷卻散熱技術 、 頂部散熱與 Clip 
等產品 。 由於 AI 伺服器功率密度大幅提高 ， 高壓直流電 （ HVDC）
架構逐漸普及，使功率元件與散熱需求同步提升，也將帶動界霖
產品結構升級與毛利率優化。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 4.39 元： 
展望 2026 年 ， 受惠新產品陸續放量出貨及反映銅價上漲 ， 預估 2026
年營收 60.4 億元，YoY+12.9%，在產品組合改善及高毛利產品比
重提升帶動下，預估 2026 年毛利率上升至 18.06%，同時受惠稼
動率回升與自動化效益提升，固定成本攤提改善，整體獲利成長
幅度將優於營收成長，營業利益 5.6 億元，YoY+138.3%，營益率
9.33%，稅後淨利 4.5 億元，YoY+187%，EPS 4.39 元。

## Document 094: 6291沛亨 第一金

Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

周建霖 
(886-2)2563-1666 
mark.chou@firstsec.com.tw

沛亨(6291) 
買進, TP 700 (潛在報酬 42.28%)  
買進 
Price(05/21):492 
Target Price：700

光纖市場的供需緊張持續加劇， Nvidia 也意識到此一情境的緊
迫，因此先前宣布將入股 Corning 並合作擴增北美當地光纖及連
接器產能 。 沛亨即便在握有相對充足的原料庫存下仍僅能滿足客戶
不到一半需求的訂單 ， 為此公司也上調年底擴產目標 ， 由原先2025
年產量的 3 倍上修至 5 倍。綜合以上，研究部預估沛亨 2026 年
EPS 16.62 元，2027 年 EPS 27.82 元，目前 PE 為 17.6x，考量
光纖市場的供需緊張情境持續加劇 ， 另外沛亨在相對應的產能規劃
也不斷提高，因此上調 PER 至 25x，目標價 700 元，建議買進。  
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
沛亨半導體(6291)成立於 1992 年 ， 一開始本業為類比IC 設計廠，
2018 年加入振曜集團，目前產品分為網通產品（電源管理 IC、網
路產品）和電腦周邊（揚聲器、光纖）。其中光纖產品自 2022 年
收購超昱光電後才加入業務，產品主要交給資料中心用光纖 、跳
線，上游原材料來自於美、日系供應商。2025 年產品組合電腦週
邊 65%、網通產品 35%。

 ⚫ 近況更新 
光纖出貨續強，毛利率短期受工作天數影響 
沛亨 26Q1 營收 6.6 億元 ，Q+10.6%，Y+49.5%， 毛利率37.50%，
稅後淨利 1.3 億元，Q+3.5%，Y+186.9%，EPS 3.00 元。電腦及
周邊設備占比已來到 70%，顯現光纖產品的出貨動能強勁，稼動
率持續攀升，產品組合相對優化，然而面臨農曆新年工作天數較
少，為了能滿足客戶需求的訂單，公司加班趕工所產生較多的相關
成本使毛利率暫時性下滑，費用方面則控制得宜，因此本業獲利持
續攀升。

光纖稼動率持續攀升，營運表現有望續創新高 
沛亨 4 月營收 2.4 億元，M-3.5%，Y+42.1%。光纖延續去年底來
的強勁動能 ， 公司表示目前整體光纖市場供給緊張態勢不僅沒有緩
解甚至更加嚴峻，已觀察到光纖價格較去年成長了倍數之上，然而
在擁有充足的庫存和提前備貨的情況下 ， 仍會持續努力提升稼動率
來滿足客戶需求 。 綜合以上 ， 研究部預估沛亨26Q2 營收 7.9 億元 ，
Q+20.2%，Y+53.5%，毛利率 40.87%，稅後淨利 1.5 億元，
Q+18.0%，Y+976.3%，EPS 3.54 元。

全球光纖供給緊張，沛亨上調擴產目標至 5 倍 
已明顯感受到光纖供給的緊缺 ，Nvidia 於 5/6 公告預付 5 億美元認
股權取得 300 萬股 Corning 的股份 ，加計 180 美元 1500 萬股的追
加認購權，總計最高達 32 億美元的投資 。根據雙方合作協議，
Corning 將於北卡羅來納州與德州新建三座製造廠，提升在美國本
土連接器產能 10 倍以及 50%的光纖產能 。 由此可見在未來世代下
Nvidia 的平台也將逐步提高對於光通訊傳輸的依賴 ， 光纖的使用場
景將逐步從 Scale-Across 到 Scale-Out，甚至隨著 CPO 的導入在
Scale-UP 市場的滲透率快速攀升。相比於前次法說會，目前沛亨

在手訂單已談到 27Q1，然而公司仍僅能達成不足客戶一半的需
求 ， 因此決議再進一步提高今年底的擴產幅度目標 ， 將從原先2025
年產量的 3 倍提升至 5 倍 ， 此外先前公司也已公告將進行私募進行
增資，以滿足原料及擴產等相關支出，研究部持續正向看待光纖市
場在至少近一兩年內仍屬於賣方市場 ， 且在沛亨的競爭優勢及上下
游緊密的關係下，仍將能達成優於產業成長的營運表現。綜合以
上，研究部預估沛亨 2026 年營收 32.6 億元，Y+53.3%，毛利率
41.13%，稅後淨利 7.0 億元，Y+151.8%，EPS 16.62 元；2027
年營收 46.2 億元，Y+41.5%，毛利率 44.10%，稅後淨利 11.7 億
元，Y+67.4%，EPS 27.82 元。

結論 
光纖市場的供需緊張持續加劇 ，Nvidia 也意識到此一情境的緊迫，
因此先前宣布將入股 Corning 並合作擴增北美當地光纖及連接器
產能 。 沛亨即便在握有相對充足的原料庫存下仍僅能滿足客戶不到
一半需求的訂單，為此公司也上調年底擴產目標，由原先 2025 年
產量的 3 倍上修至 5 倍 。 綜合以上 ， 研究部預估沛亨2026 年 EPS 
16.62 元，2027 年 EPS 27.82 元，目前PE 為 17.6x，考量光纖市
場的供需緊張情境持續加劇 ， 另外沛亨在相對應的產能規劃也不斷
提高，因此上調 PER 至 25x，目標價 700 元，建議買進。

6957 裕慶-KY(Horizon)

報告日期： 2026/05/22 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 1Q26 稅後 EPS 1.51 元 
在主要客戶 1Q26 積極出貨帶動，裕慶 KY 1Q26 營收金額 10.08
億元， YoY+28.64% ，QoQ-12.37% ，受到 1~2 月仍有高額關稅影
響，營業利益 1.01 億元， YoY+65.33% ，QoQ-68.78% ，稅後盈
餘 0.83 億元， YoY+29.03% ，QoQ-68.52% ，稅後 EPS 1.51 元。 
二. 預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 3.48 元 
裕慶 KY 04/2026 營收金額 3.68 億元 ，YoY+53.31% ，MoM-26.6% 。 
在商用展示架客戶訂單持續回溫，預估裕慶 KY 2Q26 營收金額
11.34 億元， YoY+40.9% ，QoQ+12.5% ，預估營業利益 2.38 億
元， YoY+516.67% ，QoQ+135.4% ，預估稅後盈餘 1.93 億元，
YoY+414.86% ，QoQ+134.08% ，預估稅後 EPS 3.48 元。  
三 . 預估商用展示架 2026 年營收金額 43 億元，
YoY+24.64% 
商用展示架 2026 年營運成長動能： 1，美國家居裝修零售量販店
在自動灑水展示架出貨量增加，預估貢獻 2026 年營收金額 8~8.5
億元， YoY+42~51% 。2，美國折扣零售百貨持續增加原有門市汰
舊換新訂單，加上新店展示架訂單，預期貢獻 2026 年營收金額
6~6.5 億元， YoY+130~170% 。3，美國跨國零售公司，在零售賣
場的展示架翻新計畫，新據點展示架，加上客戶將商示架訂單集
中至裕慶 ， 預計2026 年貢獻營收金額 4.5~5 億元 ，YoY+42~59% 。
預估商用展示架 2026 年營收 43 億元， YoY+24.64% 。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 13.00 元 
裕慶 KY 2026 年在商用展示架新客戶如美國家居裝修零售量販
店，美國折扣零售百貨與新客戶訂單挹注，帶動裕慶 KY 2026 年
獲利恢成長態勢 。 宏遠預估裕慶 KY 2026 年營收金額 46.00 億元 ，
YoY+20.3% ，預估營業利益 9.08 億元， YoY+28.76% ，預估稅後
盈餘 7.22 億元， YoY+28.76% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。  
五. 投資建議買進，目標價 208 元 
觀察裕慶 2026 年 ROE25% ，PER 12.3X ，ROE-PER 評價偏低，  
1Q~2Q26 營運呈現年增，有助評價提升，故投資建議買進，目標
價 208 元。 (16X2026 年稅後 EPS )

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
裕慶金屬成立於 1998 年，資本額 5.55 億元，並於 2024 年掛牌
上市。裕慶早期主要以展示架產品，提供全球大型零售通路商，
作為商品陳設。之後更跨足服飾、飲料、飯店、製鞋、油漆、傢
俱等全球領導品牌的商用展示架；此外，居家生活用的電動床，
其內部精密的金屬結構與製造，也得到客戶的認同，成為全球獨
家製造廠。更立基於此，近年投入資源，透過電子設備提供更有
價值的訊息，為居家生活提供更完整的加值服務。

圖一、裕慶公司沿革

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

二. 投資架構與生產基地概況  
開曼裕慶旗下有五間子公司。台灣分公司位於台灣南投，主要以
營運總部，生產製造，南投廠占整體產能約 10~15% 。郁智康，
主要以電子裝置零組件開發為主，主要用於智能電動床機構件。
蘇州裕慶主要為生產製造為主，蘇州廠占整體產能約 85~90% 。
環宇木業，為裕慶 2017 年收購，主要以木質生產基地為主，搭配
裕慶原有金屬材質商用展示架。美國芝加哥子公司  Beeline 之設
計團隊與在地倉儲服務。

圖二、裕慶投資組織架構

三. 2026 年營運概況及展望  
裕慶主要從事各類材質商用展示架與智能電動床機件之研發，生
產及銷售，展場設計服務，與居家健康管理智能模組研發等。

圖三、裕慶主要產品—商用展示架與智能電動床機構件

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

預估商用展示架 2026 年營收金額 43 億元， YoY+24.64%

商用展示架產業具有高度競爭市場，其競爭情況受多個因素影
響，包括生產要素的價格波動、產品與消費需求波動、供應鏈變
化、創新和全球化等不同因素，環環相扣。裕慶生產各類材質的
商用展示架，從設計、工程結構以及製造多年經驗，客戶主要為
北美知名品牌或零售業者 ， 客戶包括 Adidas、 BOOT BARN 、Coca 
Cola、DICKS、HOME DEPOT 、H&M、KOHLS 、LOWES 、MUJI 、 
TARGET 、UNIQLO 與 Walmart 等。

### Page 4

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

4

商用展示架主要市場在美國，其中中國出口至美國關稅課徵高額
關稅，加上台幣升值影響，許多客戶訂單呈現減少情況，但其中
不乏逆勢成長客戶，包括： 1，美國家居裝修零售量販店在自動灑
水展示架出貨量持續增加，加上拖拉車訂單挹注，貢獻裕慶 2025
年營收金額 5.6 億元， YoY+102% 。2，美國跨國零售公司，在零
售賣場的展示架翻新計畫 ， 貢獻裕慶 2025 年營收金額 3.15 億元 ，
呈現倍數成長。 3，美國折扣零售百貨集團訂單己於 4Q24 小量出
貨， 2025 年開始放量， 2025 年貢獻裕慶營收金額 1.56 億元，呈
現倍增。商用展示架 2025 年營收金額 34.50 億元， YoY+4.2% 。

商用展示架 2026 年營運成長動能包括： 1，美國家居裝修零售量
販店在自動灑水展示架出貨量持續增加 ， 預估貢獻裕慶 2026 年營
收金額 8~8.5 億元， YoY+42~51% 。2，美國折扣零售百貨持續增
加原有門市汰舊換新訂單，另外也有新店展示架訂單，預期貢獻
2026 年營收金額 6~6.5 億元， YoY+130~170% 。3，美國跨國零
售公司，在零售賣場的展示架翻新計畫，新據點展示架，加上客
戶將商示架訂單集中至裕慶，預計 2026 年貢獻裕慶營收金額
4.5~5 億元， YoY+42~59% 。4，裕慶新業務方面，己取國內某居
家門鎖公司 60% 股權，初期 2026 年客戶將以大型零售通路與電
商通路為主，預估貢獻 2026 年營收金額 0.5~1 億元。綜合上述，
宏遠預估商用展示架 2026 年營收金額 43.00 億元 ，YoY+24.64%。

預估智能電動床機構件 2026 年營收金額 3 億元， YoY-19.8%

智能電動床主要消費市場在於歐美地區，智能電動床最初鎖定消
費者為行動不便的老年人口，消費族群絕大多數為 50 歲以上人
口，但隨著消費生活品質提升，消費結構的升級與業者的推廣，
越來愈多年輕族群開始購買智能電動床，根據國際睡眠品質協會
統計，智能電動床美國地區銷售量由 2016 年的 105.09 萬張成長
至 2022 年的 207.83 萬張，年複合成長率 12.04% 。隨著互聯網
普及與消費習慣提升後，智能電動床將逐漸吞食一般床的市場份
額，其市場仍具有成長空間。裕慶智能電動床機構件主要客戶為
美國 Leggett ＆Platt 。

裕慶 2025 年，智能電動床客戶在美國關稅因素，減少訂單，受到
主要客戶訂單減少影響 ， 預估智能電動床機構件 2025 年營收金額
3.74 億元， YoY-46.88%

預期 2026 年，在美國關稅成本增加，導致民間消費通膨，民眾將
消費將以必需品為主，非必要品將會被排擠，恐不利智能電動床
銷售 ， 預估智能電動床機構件 2026 年營收金額 3 億元 ，YoY-19.8%

元

裕慶 KY 2026 年在商用展示架新客戶如美國家居裝修零售量販
店，美國折扣零售百貨與新客戶訂單挹注，帶動裕慶 KY 2026 年
獲利恢成長態勢 。 宏遠預估裕慶 KY 2026 年營收金額 46.00 億元 ，
YoY+20.3% ，預估營業利益 9.08 億元， YoY+28.76% ，預估稅後
盈餘 7.22 億元， YoY+28.76% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。

公告配發現金股利 9 元，現金殖利率 5.6%

KY 股利政策，配發現金股利 13.16 元，股利發放率 92% 。裕慶
2025 年股利政策，將配發現金股利 9 元，現金殖利率 5.6% 。

個股報告 
新代(7750) 
轉單效應與市佔擴張雙軌並進 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $2960 
前次投資建議 2025.12.15： 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價: $1060

報告日期：2026.05.25 研究員：陳威仲

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 基於 26Q1 營收與獲利皆超預期，且看好中長期新代持續擴大中國市占率，與2027 年馬來
西亞新廠完工後，海外新興市場帶來之成長性，目標價由 1060 元(23 x 26(F)PER)調整至
2960 元(35 x 26(F)PER)，維持【逢低買進】投資評等。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/05/22 收盤價： 2,390.0 ➢ 26Q1 營收 48.7 億元，QoQ+31.7%/YoY+49.5%，大幅優
於兆豐預期 25.6%；毛利率 48.8%，季增 3.0/年增 4.2個
百分點，優於兆豐前次預期 4.2 個百分點，主因高階控制
器營收占比提升，與主要來自於同業缺料、漲價所帶來的
短期高毛利溢價與規模經濟；稅後淨利 10.7 億元，
YoY+123.0%，EPS 15.4 元，優於前次預期的 7.5元。 
➢ 受惠於高階自動化市場需求回溫，新代目前控制器主要競
爭對手(如西門子、三菱等)面臨需求激增之下，同時記憶
體與 IC 元件等控制器外購材料也面臨預期持續上漲之
下，使同業控制器宣布漲價之外， 交期也大幅拉長 (由
25Q4 的 1個月拉長至約 3個月)，迎來轉單效應。 
➢ 新代集團下的聯達智能 2025 年機械手臂銷量突破 1000
台，而 2026 年 1~5月，聯達手臂結合雲端方案的營收年
增率約為 40~50%，子公司事業事業同步成長。 
➢ 預估新代 2026 年稅後淨利為 60.8 億元，YoY+166.2%，
EPS84.6 元，目前股價位於 28 x26(F)PER，位於過去
25~36X 的下緣，基於 26Q1 營收與獲利皆超預期，且看
好中長期新代持續擴大中國市占率，與 2027 年馬來西亞
新廠完工後，海外新興市場帶來之成長性，目標價由1060
元(23 x 26(F)PER)調整至 2960 元(35 x 26(F)PER)，維
持【逢低買進】投資評等，潛在上漲空間 23.9%。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐國際預估 
營 運 分 析

新代科技為亞太市場精密機械控制器領導廠商 
新代科技股份有限公司成立於 1995 年，專注於精密機械控制器領域，主要
銷售 CNC 控制器、伺服馬達，並積極開發工業型機械手臂、數控雲端軟體
與自動化設備解決方案等，持續提升產品競爭力與營運多角化。新代客戶多
為工具機與精密加工設備廠商，終端應用產業約為汽車(30%)、刀具模具與
通用機械(25%)、3C(15%)、其他(30%)，目前新代於中國控制器市場市佔率
已達約 25%，隨後則為發那科(FANUC)的 20%，新代近年已成為亞太市場
精密機械控制器領導廠商。

26Q1 自動化控制器需求激增帶動新代營收與獲利創高 
新代 26Q1 營收 48.7 億元，QoQ+31.7%/YoY+49.5%，大幅優於兆豐預期
25.6%，主因 25Q4 起中國自動化控制器需求激增，交期拉長與同業漲價之
下，轉單效應顯著；毛利率 48.8%，季增 3.0/年增 4.2 個百分點，優於兆豐
前次預期 4.2 個百分點，主因高階控制器營收占比提升，與主要來自於同業
缺料、漲價所帶來的短期高毛利溢價與規模經濟 ；稅後淨利 10.7 億元，
YoY+123.0%，EPS 15.4 元，優於前次預期的 7.5 元。

同業交期拉長創造轉單紅利，定價策略與成本控管穩固市占率 
受惠於高階自動化市場需求回溫，新代目前控制器主要競爭對手(如西門子、
三菱等)面臨需求激增之下，同時記憶體與 IC 元件等控制器外購材料也面臨
預期持續上漲之下，使同業控制器宣布漲價之外，交期也大幅拉長(由 25Q4
的 1個月拉長至約 3個月)。而中國正值工業設備汰舊換新之週期，若下游工
業客戶不趁現在汰換或購置控制器，在預期原料持續上漲且產業交期持續拉
長之下，若遇到控制器的無貨可發，將面對產線的停機風險與大幅的損失。
而新代相較於同業選擇漲價，初期仍選擇穩定報價，除了創造目前高階控制
器的持續轉單，也利於在中國市占率的持續擴張。 
而針對市場擔憂的 DRAM 記憶體漲價風險，目前主要的挑戰在於料件的取
得難度，新代已採取多供應商策略以確保料源無虞，預期記憶體成本上升的
遞延效應將落在 26Q2~26Q3 浮現，但由於軟體與主板設計為新代 100%自
製，外部零組件成本的衝擊在可控範圍內，即便未將成本完全轉嫁給客戶，
仍預期能維持公司整體 45%的長期目標毛利率。 
兆豐預估新代在交期速度與 ASP競爭力皆優於同業，在成本可控之下，高階
控制器產品將持續受惠轉單效益。目前全球 AI製造鏈原料成本皆持續上漲之
下，預估自動化設備需求端與成本端推動的螺旋上漲將持續，且印度與東南
亞等新興市場營收增速持續優於公司整體水準，隨著 2027 年馬來西亞新廠
啟用，預估將帶動新代 2026~2027 年控制器出貨套數年增率維持在高雙位數
水準。

百萬元

子公司聯達智能與正鉑雷射隨新代控制器的市占上升同步成長 
隨著製造業終端朝向智慧彈性製造轉型，新代集團下的聯達智能透過深度整
合 CNC 與機械手臂的策略，2025 年機械手臂銷量突破 1000 台，而 2026
年 1~5月，聯達手臂結合雲端方案的營收年增率約為 40~50%，顯示從單機
智慧走向單元自動化的策略正快速變現。且新代與聯達於 4/27日正式與中國
華沿機器人簽署戰略合作備忘錄，確立了在三年內共同推動 10,000 台協作
機器人的銷售目 標。而子公司正鉑雷射正積極將 既有的工廠自動化統包方
案，向上延伸至高附加價值的半導體設備領域。其中除了再生晶圓雷射除膜
技術之外，針對特殊材料(如玻璃)進行表面標記與改質的技術能量，結合其
在切割與精微加工的深厚基礎，正鉑針對玻璃基板所開發的雷射鑽孔與微加
工設備，有望順應未來先進封裝與高階載板材料升級的產業趨勢。

- 5 - 
個股報告 
預估新代中國市占率持續上升，且海外市場與子公司表現同步轉強 
兆豐預估 2026 年新代將持續因中國市占率的提升與轉單效益，而使控制器
事業持續維持高雙位數增長 ，且海外市場與子公司仍在基期較低的 爆發階
段，預估新代 2026 年營收為 264.2 億元，YoY+89.2%；惟預估 26Q1 較高
毛利為短期的高階控制器營收占比急升所致，而隨著蘇州二廠於 26Q2 完工
啟用後，且記憶體與 IC 元件的持續上漲，將對毛利產生部分壓力， 預估
26Q2~26H2 將逐步反應成本的上升，使毛利率回到 45~47%的區間。 
預估新代 2026 年稅後淨利為 60.8 億元，YoY+166.2%，EPS84.6 元，目前
股價位於 28 x26(F)PER，位於過去 25~36X的下緣，基於 26Q1 營收與獲利
皆超預期，且看好中長期新代持續擴大中國市占率，與 2027 年馬來西亞新
廠完工後，海外新興市場帶來之成長性，目標價由 1060 元(23 x 26(F)PER)
調整至 2960元(35 x 26(F)PER)，維持【逢低買進】投資評等，潛在上漲空
間 23.9%。

公司簡介 
翔名為半導體設備零組件代工廠 ， 成立初期
以代理日商幫浦產品 、 石墨承載盤為主要業
務，2023 年併購芝和精密，切入前段蝕刻
設備所用電極板 、 矽環等 ， 目前主要透過精
密加工、表面處理、雷熱熔覆、電子束焊接
等技術生產如石墨及金屬件 、 合金件等半導
體設備零組件的製造翻修 ， 現為台灣半導體
用離子植入機最大代工廠。

主要客戶： 美商應用材料 (AMAT US) 、
ASML、晶圓製造廠等 
主要競爭對手：

王彥鈞 stanley.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：座談會

翔名 (8091 TT) 
產業成長、業務拓展

永豐觀點 
1Q26 營運優於預期，主因成本轉嫁和低價庫存帶動獲利提升。研究部預期翔名受
惠半導體產業上升週期，對設備、耗材需求，在 2026-2027 年營運持續創高。 
 投資評價與建議 
目標價升至 271 元：翔名 1Q26 營運創高且 2-3Q26 有望續揚，短期受惠半導體
及航太專案、適度轉嫁原料成本而成長，中期亦受惠半導體製造廠產能利用率提
升、全球擴產，帶動設備及耗材的直接/間接需求。研究部維持翔名買進建議，目
標價則升至 271 元(25x 2027 EPSF)。

### Page 2

2

翔名為半導體設備零組件代工廠：翔名成立於 1991 年，2004 年上櫃，資本額約
5.27 億，成立初期以代理日商幫浦產品、石墨承載盤為主要業務，2023 年併購芝
和精密，切入前段蝕刻設備所用電極板、矽環等，目前主要透過精密加工、表面處
理、雷熱熔覆、電子束焊接等技術生產如石墨及金屬件、合金件等半導體設備零組
件的製造翻修，現為台灣半導體用離子植入機最大代工廠。總部位於新竹香山，生
產據點則位在新竹、南京，並在日本熊本、嘉義馬稠後有擴廠計畫。主要客戶為美
商應用材料(AMAT US)、ASML、晶圓製造廠等。 
1Q26 營收、獲利雙雙創歷史紀錄： 翔名 1Q26 營收 6.5 億(+16.9%QoQ，
+38.0%YoY)， 其中半導體相關業務成長8%QoQ、 光電相關業務成長286%QoQ，
分別受惠於(1) 特化管路通過驗證，於 1Q26 開始放量，(2) 開始將貴金屬漲價成
本反映給客戶，和(3) 專案出貨認列，毛利率達 40.6%的新高水準，係因較好的產
品組 合 、匯 率 、即 啟動 漲 價以 反 映成 本 ，稅 後淨利 1.3 億(+33.1%QoQ，
+107.8%YoY)，EPS 2.56 元，大幅優於研究部預期的 1.4 元。 
隨產業、新專案放量：研究部預期翔名 2026 年將隨(1) 晶圓代工廠產能利用率回
升(先進製程滿載，成熟製程陸續回到 80%以上)，帶動耗材需求，(2) 設備原廠客
戶需求提升 ，主要客戶先前在法說預期全年半導體設備銷售年增30%YoY， 且將與
供應鏈共同擴張產能，(3) 2024-2025 年所接的航太訂單於 2026 年陸續認列，(4) 
鎢 、 鉬等貴金屬成本在2025 年持續墊高 ， 翔名自2026 年起陸續反映成本給客戶 ，
預期 3Q26 將進行第二波價格調整 ，(4) 新專案如特化管路用於先進製程化學品供
應系統、異質焊接、陶瓷度膜、光學反射器用於爐管設備中等，預期2026 年整體
新專案營收貢獻將翻倍。 
廠務調整中： (1) 熊本廠已於 4/15 開幕，目前客戶正在驗證產線，後續有望在
4Q26-1Q27 起貢獻營收，(2) 嘉義廠維持 2027 年底完工之預期，因應蝕刻設備
客戶、子公司等需求，自2028 年起投產。 (3) 南京廠持續增加投入，以滿足在地
化客戶對半導體耗材需求，2026 年可維持雙位數成長。 
財務預估：研究部預估翔名 2026 年營收 28.6 億(+33.8%YoY)，毛利率 37.8%，
稅後淨利 4.9 億(+61.4%YoY)，EPS 9.32 元(原估 8.47 元)。2027 年營收 32.5 億
(+13.9%YoY)，毛利率 39.3%，稅後淨利 5.8 億(+17.8%YoY)，EPS 10.86 元。

• After an extended bearish stance, where EM have been on a relative downtrend
ever since 2008, we turned OW EM equities last year, and argued in our ’26

Year Ahead that this should be their second consecutive year of outperformance.
We refresh arguments over why EM are likely to have a further meaningful
rally in 2H of the year:
• 1. AI trade continuing . In March we highlighted that Mag-7 moved to the
cheapest in 10 years, and AI at risk stocks became record cheap. The attractive
valuations, underpinned by strong earnings, helped the space rally. EM AI plays
are in our view even cheaper and offer further upside potential, and represent a

big chunk of the overall index, at 40% of MSCI EM. Our EM strategists stay
bullish on Korea and Taiwan.  Here, memory rally has been aggressive, but
meaningful supply additions are not arriving before 2H of next year, with
equities typically discounting the worsening supply-demand balance 6-9
months ahead, so it would be too early to turn bearish already. We also believe
that China Tech should catch up, as well, and it is trading at near record discounts

to others.
• 2. Fed, bond yields and USD view. We continue to believe that market pricing
of central banks’ hawkishness, which has spiraled since the Iran war started, is
likely to reverse in 2H - differences to 2022 remain dramatic , in terms of
corporate pricing power, wage growth, real rates, etc. - see our report. With a new
Fed chairman arriving, the wildcard is that some of the Fed’s reaction function
shifts more dovish later in the year. Bond yields could drift lower in 2H, either

way the conflict proceeds from here. USD has acted as a safe haven in the past
2 months, but we note that it is trading expensive in a long-term context, to the
tune of 10-15%. If USD is softer, EM tended to benefit.
• 3. China green shoots? In our Year Ahead, we argued that China activity was
likely to inflect higher, given the liquidity infusion, supportive policy stance and
a stabilization in house prices. We are seeing increasing evidence of this. The

upcoming Trump-Xi meeting could aid the outlook.
• 4. Iran conflict to subside. We have consistently argued since the second half
of March to use the dips driven by geopolitical uncertainty to buy into. We
continue to see bouts of weakness as opportunities to add, and don’t find equities
as complacent, despite fresh all-time highs for both MXWO and for MXEF. If
the geopolitical situation stabilizes in 2H, with easing concerns over oil supplies,
EM stand to benefit.

• 5. Valuations, positioning and flows are all attractive for EM. EM P/E relative
to DM is trading at record cheap at present - middle chart, and at absolute 12x
forward P/E the space is far from demanding. Investor positioning remains low
in EM, and flows are accelerating - bottom chart.
• We stay very bullish on Mining, Semis and Industrials  sectors. Cyclical
Consumer plays are trading at relative lows, which likely stays the case until the
Iran conflict shows more concrete evidence of waning, but are likely to have a

Equity Strategy

After an extended bearish stance on Emerging markets, where
EM equities were on a relative downtrend ever since 2018,
we turned Overweight EM equities last year and argued in

our 2026 Year Ahead that this should be their second consec-
utive year of outperformance. Since the beginning of 2025,
EM equities have cumulatively outperformed DM by approx-
imately 25% in dollar terms. However, this gain remains
modest compared to the 47% relative underperformance that
EM experienced versus DM from 2018 to 2024, suggesting
the reversal has considerably further to run.
Figure 2: MSCI EM vs DM

MSCI EM relative to DM ($)
Source: Datastream
The multi-year underperformance of EM versus DM from
2018 to 2024 was one of the longest and deepest relative
drawdowns on record. This was mainly driven by a strong
dollar, China's structural slowdown, and the dominance of US
mega-cap technology sector. We believe the conditions that
sustained this underperformance are now reversing, the dollar

is expensive and likely to weaken, China policy is turning
supportive, and EM AI plays offer a valuation-attractive alter-
native to crowded US tech positions. The 2025 outperfor-
mance of 9% and the 2026 YTD gain of approximately 15%
represent, in our view, only the early stages of a multi-year
mean reversion.

MSCI EM vs MSCI DM ($) Iran Attack

Despite the disruption caused by the Iran conflict in Q1, EM
equities have demonstrated resilience, with MSCI EM mak-
ing fresh all-time highs alongside MSCI World. We do not
find equities complacent at these levels, given the supportive
fundamental backdrop and the degree of underinvestment that
persists in the asset class.
In this report, we refresh our arguments over why EM is like-
ly to have a further meaningful rally in the second half of the

year.
1)  EM offers cheaper exposure to the resurgent AI trade

In March, we highlighted that Mag-7 valuations had moved to
the cheapest level in 10 years on a relative P/E basis, and that
AI-at-risk stocks had become record cheap. Attractive valua-

tions, underpinned by strong earnings delivery, have helped
the space rally from the lows.

One of the key concerns around the AI trade earlier was the
difficulty in monetisation. The worry was that, with no clear
moat in AI services, value creation would accrue predomi-

nantly to consumers rather than providers, eventually leading
to a reassessment of spending plans. Recent developments
have materially shifted this debate. Anthropic's disclosed rev-
enue acceleration, from approximately $9bn ARR at the end
of 2025 to approximately $30bn ARR in April 2026, has reig-
nited the bullish AI trade. Token consumption growth has
accelerated from approximately 10x year-on-year at end 2025
to approximately 20x currently, and we are seeing a modera-

tion of pricing deflation with various approaches to throttle
token usage and increase pricing with new model launches.
Figure 6: High Bandwidth Memory Revenue (HBM)
4.1 16.9

HBM Revenue ($bn)
Source: J.P. Morgan. Korea Technology team
Beyond the improving monetization picture, tightness in the
AI compute supply chain is filtering through to pricing
increases across the board. The memory rally has been

aggressive, with DRAM prices more than tripling from their
2023 trough. Samsung Electronics posted an eightfold
increase in quarterly profit, with chips responsible for 94% of
its record total, while SK Hynix reported a fivefold increase
in quarterly earnings. Together, these two companies account
for almost 55% of MSCI Korea.
However, meaningful supply additions are not arriving before
the second half of 2027. New fab capacity from Samsung and
SK Hynix is not expected to come online until late next year

at the earliest, and the conversion of existing DRAM lines to
HBM further constrains conventional memory supply. Equi-
ties typically discount a worsening supply demand balance 6
to 9 months ahead of the physical market turning, which sug-
gests it would be too early to turn bearish on memory names
already. Better risk management, in our view, may be in being
slightly late rather than too early in positioning for a cycle
downturn, an approach we have advocated for in Korea over

the past six months.
This is a meaningful positive for the entire AI hardware sup-
ply chain, which is overwhelmingly domiciled in EM Asia.
Figure 7: US Hyperscaler Capex spend ($ billion)

US tech giants are expected to spend close to $700 billion in
capex this year, much of it directed toward AI infrastructure.

Nvidia now relies on Asian suppliers for approximately 90%
of its production costs, up from 65% last year. The tech capex
outlook is likely to be revised even higher on the back of
recent cybersecurity-risk-related headlines, as the private and
public sectors actively look to minimize system vulnerabili-
ties and modernise legacy infrastructure.

The Asia Tech hardware ecosystem sits at the centre of the
global AI buildout, and our colleagues see the supply demand
tightness persisting for at least the next two to three quarters,
supporting continued pricing power across the chain.

Critically, AI-related names represent a substantial portion of

Korea earnings momentum remains exceptionally strong,
with MSCI Korea forward EPS continuing to see upward

as the valuation discount narrows.

Within the Asian AI stack, Korea has led by a wide margin,
but Taiwan companies are now catching up. The MSCI Tai-

China Tech is trading at a meaningful discount and
should catch up

We also believe that China Tech should catch up. The KWEB
(China Internet) index is down almost 10% year to date,
underperforming MSCI China year to date even as Tech con-
tinues to do better globally.
Figure 13: Global token consumption capture by Open router
Source: J.P. Morgan. China Internet Team, OpenRouter (data as of April 13, 2026)
The China AI ecosystem is experiencing explosive growth.
Average daily token consumption in China exceeded 140 tril-

lion in March 2026, compared to just 100 billion at the start
of 2024.  China now has over 600 million generative AI users,
a 142% increase from the prior year.
DeepSeek's recent release of its V4 model is optimised for
domestic chips, particularly Huawei's Ascend 950, triggering
a surge in demand from major tech firms including
ByteDance, Tencent, and Alibaba. This accelerates the indi-
genisation theme and reduces China's vulnerability to US
export controls, which we view as a structural positive for the

domestic semiconductor supply chain.

The market continues to apply an excessive geopolitical risk
premium to Chinese technology names in our opinion. While
US-China tensions remain a structural overhang, we note that
China's AI ecosystem is increasingly self-sufficient. Any sta-

bilisation in US-China relations, including the upcoming
Trump-Xi meeting, could serve as a catalyst for re-rating. We
see China Tech as offering asymmetric upside from current
levels.
2. The hawkish pricing of Fed policy is likely to reverse

We continue to believe that market pricing of central bank
hawkishness, which spiralled since the Iran conflict started, is
likely to reverse in the second half of the year. Markets
moved ECB rate forecasts up by as much as 70bp since the
initial escalation, and taken out expectations for Fed cuts by
almost 60bp. Pricing of end-2026 Fed funds futures remains
well above pre-conflict levels. We believe this hawkish
repricing is set to unwind.

Table 3: Iran Conflict vs Russia - Ukraine (2022)
Category Pre - Iran Conflict 2022
US CPI Deaccelerating (3.3% currently) Elevated and accelerating (9.1% at the peak in June '22)
US Core CPI Deaccelerating (2.6% currently) Elevated and accelerating (6.6% at the peak in June '22)

Fed Policy Rate Hold at 3.75% Start hiking from 0.25%
Source: J.P. Morgan.
While an extended conflict could push prices higher in the
near term, the differences to 2022 remain dramatic. Wage
growth is decelerating rather than accelerating, corporate

pricing power is fading, real rates are already restrictive, and
the oil intensity of GDP is roughly 60% lower than in the
1970s. Central banks entered 2022 with policy rates well
below neutral and were forced to catch up aggressively. At
present, policy rates are largely in line with historical norms.
With a new Fed chairman arriving later this year, the wild
card is that some of the Fed's reaction function shifts more
dovish. Bond yields could drift lower in the second half

regardless of how the conflict proceeds from here, as the
growth impulse from the oil shock fades and the disinflation-
ary forces in services and wages reassert themselves.
Figure 16: US 10Y bond yield

US 10Y bond yield Iran attack
Source: Bloomberg Finance L.P.
Bond yields across DM have remained elevated, 40-50bp
higher versus pre-conflict levels, altogether tightening finan-

cial conditions. We believe this represents an overshoot that
will correct as the conflict de-escalates and inflation expecta-
tions normalise. For EM assets, a decline in DM yields would
ease funding costs, support capital flows, and reduce the
opportunity cost of holding EM equities.
Figure 17: Current deviation of average REER PPI/CPI vs 15yr moving
average
Source: J.P. Morgan. FX Strategy
The dollar has acted as a safe haven in the past two months,

but we note that it is trading expensive in a long-term context,
to the tune of 10-15% above fair value on our FX team's
REER models. Our FX strategists' longer-term bearish dollar
view remains intact. They believe the dollar outlook is less
bearish than it was pre-conflict due to persistent energy pres-
sures, but it is not sufficient to alter the medium-term bearish
stance.

While consumption indicators remain subdued, we note that
the policy emphasis is shifting toward consumption-focused
measures, including expanded trade-in subsidies, pension
increases, birth incentives, and childcare support. The 15th
Five Year Plan explicitly calls for a "notable increase in the
consumption rate," and our economists expect fiscal support
for consumption to rise to approximately 700 billion yuan
(0.5% of GDP) in 2026.

The CSI300 has recently hit its highest level since early 2022,

higher-value-added sectors. We encounter considerable scep-

ticism about China's growth prospects among investors, with
most not expecting material fiscal support from policymakers.
We see scope for a positive surprise.
4. EM stands to benefit from easing geopolitical risks
related to Iran

MSCI AC World ($) Iran attack
Source: Datastream
We have consistently argued from the second half of March
that we should use the dips driven by geopolitical uncertainty
to buy into. The V-shaped rebound that materialised validated

this approach, and we continue to see any weakness as oppor-
tunities to add. We do not find equities complacent despite
fresh all-time highs for both MSCI World and MSCI EM.
The path to a negotiated settlement looks more visible now,
given constraints on both sides. While the situation remains
unresolved and poses non-linear risks to commodity supply
should it persist, the range of potential outcomes has substan-
tially diminished. Energy prices have moderated from their

peaks but remain well above pre-conflict levels. However, we
see very limited risk of the current situation devolving into a
2022 style energy crisis.

Should conditions improve, EM stands to benefit dispropor-

tionately. Net energy importing economies, notably China,
India, Korea, and Taiwan, would see a direct uplift to their
terms of trade and corporate margins, amplifying the positive
impact relative to developed market peers.

The EM P/E relative to DM is trading at a record discount at
present, at 12x forward P/E versus approximately 20x for
DM, this represents a 37% discount, significantly wider than
the historical median discount. At an absolute 12x forward P/
E, the space is far from demanding, particularly given the
earnings growth trajectory of 51% for 2026 and 19% for
2027.

Investor positioning remains low in EM. Despite strong
inflows over the past year, EM equity allocations remain well
below benchmark weights. EM accounts for approximately
11% of MSCI ACWI market capitalisation but only around
6% of global AUM. This structural underweight provides a
powerful tailwind as investors gradually normalise their allo-
cations.

Equity Strategy Key Calls and Drivers
Iran conflict might need to escalate in order to de-escalate, but an oil spike and resultant market weakness might not sustain, as an escalation might in fact make an off-ramp more likely. The
considerations of swiftly rising US gasoline price and adverse polling on one side, and on the other the increasing discomfort among Iran’s largest oil customers become more acute as time passes. While

near term volatility is probable, we think that if one has a time horizon longer than next days/weeks, on 3/6/12 months timeframe one should continue using dips to add into. Now, is it obvious that there is
a disconnect between equities and commodity markets given that MXWO completed a V-shaped rebound, but at the same time Strait of Hormuz is staying closed? Not necessarily. Of course, if Brent
shows a non-linear move higher, equities will be lower. But also, similar to what transpired around Liberation day, the market can get whipsawed on any positive headline. Equities are not all that

complacent beneath the surface - current market breath is very narrow and nearly all consumer plays are lingering at lows. We see big differences to 2022 template, and do not think that stagflation is the
most likely outcome for 2H, while market earnings are staying on an uptrend, and we indicated in March that Mag-7 valuations turned outright cheap again. We looked for China green shoots - a pickup in
domestic activity, which should help our EM OW.

## Document 258: UBS China Equity Strategy How crowded is the AI trade and

Source PDF: all report pdf/UBS China Equity Strategy How crowded is the AI trade and.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-25
Pages: 24
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 21 May 2026
Global Research
China Equity Strategy
How crowded is the AI trade and other
frequently asked investor questions
Buoyant mood supported by earnings and improving fundamentals
In this note, we provide answers to five of the topical questions by investors in our recent
conversations. In general sentiment is buoyant with investors largely putting behind the
impact of Strait of Hormuz shutdown, anchored by solid earnings from hardware tech

companies. AI hardware tech seems to be the consensus long among investors with
PM's and generalists dedicating more time looking for alpha opportunities along the
supply chain though  some investors question the sustainability of the rally given
crowded positions. The property market recovery also garnered significant level of
interest from investors and while conviction level of a sustained recovery is not high, the
positive development is supportive for overall market sentiment. Another topic of

interest was whether the recent rise in PPI, driven by imported inflation, could weigh on
corporate earnings this time around. In addition, investors showed interest in the
potential re-rating of HSTECH though in general sentiment is poor around the internet
sector.
How crowded is the AI trade and what could derail the rally?
Current investor positioning of AI hardware names  is elevated but this has been the case
most of this year as evidenced by: 1) onshore mutual fund positioning in telecom and

electronics have been broadly stable in the last 3 quarters with a decline in electronics
allocation offset by higher allocation towards telecoms (Figure 2); 2) the average
crowding factor of a basket of popular AI names indicate that investor positioning has
risen this year, although there has been a modest pullback from the high recorded in
early May 2026 (Figure 3); 3) Turnover in the broader TMT sector is  elevated at ~37% of
total A-share turnover, compared with a historical peak of 43% in 2023. Looking at

regional flows and positions, foreign investor outflows from Taiwan and Korea equities
(both global AI proxies) this year  suggest some broad profit taking in this space.
Meanwhile, domestic token pricing has been rising  and while valuation is high with
semi's for example trading on 59x forward P/E (2.5 std. dev above historical average
shown in Figure 41), earnings growth in the sector has also been accelerating (up 64%
in 1Q26).  It is difficult to specifically pinpoint the events that could derail the tech rally

with some investors pointing to the rising US interest rates, IPO's of the large LLM
companies and a slowdown in earnings growth as potential sign-posts that could trigger
a re-assessment of positioning.
Would higher PPI lead to lower earnings?
Investors were concerned whether imported inflation would squeeze corporate margins
given the recent surge in energy prices. We think a higher PPI may not hurt overall listed
company earnings given the following observations: 1) higher PPI's historically have led

to higher revenue and earnings growth in listed companies. This relationship also held
up during the Russia–Ukraine war in 2022, when supply disruptions also drove a surge in
energy and transport prices; 2) exporters' earnings historically have done well in periods
of imported inflation as this gives companies an excuse to raise prices (Figure 23), 3)
listed companies generally have better ability to pass on the higher costs to customers -
historically utilities and food and beverage companies' earnings were shown to be the

### Page 2

China Equity Strategy21 May 2026 ab 2
Last month we held the tactical view (see here) that HSTECH could outperform near
term given a myriad of catalysts coming up. Many of these catalysts have come through
as we had hoped for  including: 1) launch of the new LLM models from Deepseek and
Tencent; 2) food delivery competition easing in the quarterly results, 3) US China
leadership summit promoting strategic stability and leading to H200 chip ban lift, 4) EV

demand to pick up as oil price stay higher for longer. While these development were
positive, we now think it may take longer for HSTECH to outperform:  our recent
discussions with investors suggest that their attention remains focused around hardware
tech, power equipment and other North Asian names which may have drawn liquidity
away from HK internet names. We also note upcoming lock-up expiry coming up for the
LLM companies in July - historically companies facing lock-up expiry typically

underperform in the lead-up to the expiry date and should these LLM companies be
included in HSTECH, this could also trigger some short term pressure for the index
performance. We have included a schedule of lock-up expiry in the coming months in
Figure 32.
Prefer A-share ChiNext, tech hardware and electrical equipment
We prefer A-share ChiNext given its greater exposure towards robust earnings
momentum (+23% in 1Q26) and its higher exposure towards hardware tech and power

equipment names. Our preferred sectors and model portfolio are shown in Figure 39
and 40 - we have removed the property underweight though retain our underweight in
consumers.
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

### Page 3

China Equity Strategy21 May 2026 ab 3
China Equity Strategy UBS Research
How crowded is the AI trade?
AI has remained the most widely discussed topic during our investor conversations. Most
investors acknowledged that the AI narrative is well supported by strong fundamentals.
The key concern is the degree of crowding in the AI trade and the potential downside
risk should positioning unwind. Current indicators suggest that crowding in the AI
theme is high but this has been the case for some time and broadly unchanged in recent

periods:
 Onshore mutual fund positioning: Allocation to electronics declined from a
peak of 22% in 3Q25 to 20% in 1Q26, with overweight (OW) relative to
benchmark reduced by c.2ppt. In contrast, allocations to telecom (another AI-
linked sector) rose to an all-time high of 11% as of 1Q26, representing 4ppt
overweight versus benchmark. If we combine the aggregate positioning in
electronics and telecom, positions are close to a historical high though broadly

unchanged in the last 3 quarters (show in Figure 2).
 Stock-level positioning: Crowding scores for a sample basket of popular AI
names (see list in Figure 7, list based on our previous report on China AI) indicate
that investor positioning has increased materially in the last 12 months, although
there has been a modest pullback from the highest  recorded in early May 2026.
 Market activity concentration: Turnover in the broader TMT sector has trended

higher since 2025 and remains elevated at ~37% of total A-share turnover,
compared with a historical peak of 43% in 2023.
Foreign investor flows out of Taiwan and Korea equities (both are AI proxies) in recent
months also suggest some profit taking by investors of these themes in recent periods.
Our EM team's data suggest that investors' remain underweight in the North Asian
markets.

What could derail the AI tech trade in
China?
There is no definitive answer to this question given the strong fundamentals and near
term share price momentum. Investors are seemingly looking more into the US market
for read-across for China tech - we note that the correlation between US and China
AI tech names have picked up materially this year as shown in Figure 12 and as such we
believe global spillover presents one of the biggest downside risks to the AI tech trade in

China.
There are sign-posts that investors may look to as potential pit stops in re-assessing the
AI trade, including:
 Continued rise in US yields (primarily driven by higher energy prices) though
arguably this is now somewhat reflected in the share price given the significant rise
in the US 10 year interest rate in recent periods. We note that historically the
correlation of Chinese hardware tech to US 10 year government bond yield has
been limited;

 Deceleration in earnings growth, though so far earnings growth has been robust
for the AI hardware tech space and accelerating as shown in Figure 10. This is also
the case for US tech hardware names;
 Listing of the largest LLM providers in the US. Some investors may see this as a sign
of peak growth  of LLM monetization.

For investors concerned about crowding in tech, we think a proxy could be the power

### Page 9

China Equity Strategy21 May 2026 ab 9
Would higher PPI lead to lower
earnings?
Investors are concerned as to whether imported inflation would squeeze corporate
margins as the recent surge in energy and commodity prices are primarily supply-driven.
We note that historically a higher PPI have led to faster listed companies' revenue and
earnings growth in China, even during the 2022 Russia Ukraine conflict as shown in the
following charts.
Figure 20: A share non-financials trailing earnings growth

vs PPI YoY
Source: FactSet, UBS
Figure 21: A share non-financials trailing earnings growth
vs Brent oil YoY
Source: FactSet, UBS
From a sector perspective, the following sectors historically have performed better when
PPI rose:
 Upstream sectors, including energy and capital goods, benefit the most as direct
beneficiaries of higher commodity prices;
 Export oriented sectors tend to do well with higher PPI - one would imagine that as
global commodity prices rise, all players raise prices and Chinese companies tend

to benefit from the inflationary environment;
 Sectors with stronger pricing power, such as shipping companies and consumer
durables, also tend to benefit from higher PPI;
 Midstream manufacturing sectors (e.g., chemicals, autos, and tech hardware)
historically saw moderate gains, as they are able to partially pass through cost
inflation.
 Semiconductors show little correlation with PPI given their distinct earnings
cyclicality;
 Downstream sectors with weaker pricing power, such as food & beverage,

consumer services, and utilities, were typically negatively impacted by rising input
costs. That said, these sectors make up 10% of the CSI 300 index.
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

That being said, whether China can sustainably exit deflation remains uncertain. In our
earlier analysis of Japan’s reflation episode in 2022 (see here), we highlighted
employment conditions and wage growth as key drivers in Japan’s exit from deflation.
With youth unemployment rate remaining elevated and wage growth moderating in
2H25 (based on A-share listed company data), overall labour market conditions appear
to remain lukewarm which are likely to feed into a weak broad consumption

environment near term.

### Page 12

China Equity Strategy21 May 2026 ab 12
How much impact could we see coming
from lock-up expiry?
The Hong Kong IPO market has remained active into 2026, with several large-cap
listings such as Zhipu and MiniMax successfully coming to market earlier this year.
Looking ahead, the value of shares subject to lock-up expiry is expected to rise over the
next two months, reaching a peak of c.HK$200bn in July.
Historically we see a meaningful impact from lock-up expiries at an individual stock level:

typically stocks underperform the broader market 3 months out from the lock-up expiry
date by c.25% and then  outperform post the expiry date. For the recent IPO's  in the last
18 months, the effect is less significant with stocks expecting lock-up expiry having
underperformed 6% (median) vs the HSI in the three months leading up to the lock-up
expiry.
Figure 29: HK IPO share price performance relative to HSI around 1st lock up
expiry date
Source: FactSet, Bloomberg, UBS Note: History since 2012, simple average is used

For the broader market, there is less of a correlation - the estimated HK$200bn of unlock
in July represents only around 0.4% of total HK market cap, or roughly ¾ of average
daily turnover and would therefor appear manageable.
Figure 30: Estimated monthly amount subject to IPO lock-
up expiry
Source: Bloomberg, UBS
Figure 31: HK total market cap and average daily turnover
Source: CEIC, UBS

### Page 14

China Equity Strategy21 May 2026 ab 14
What to do with HSTECH?
HSTECH is down c.10% YTD, significantly underperforming MSCI AC World by 20%
which is trading close to its all time high. We believe the key factors are:
 Unfavorable sector mix with internet, auto and consumer making up c.70% of
the index, where they are all facing different headwinds YTD, including anti-trust
regulation (against Trip.com), ongoing industry competition and absence of

consumption stimulus;
 Investors have become more selective among AI names since 2026, as the
narrative has shifted from software (seen as an AI loser) to hardware (AI enabler).
This has weighed on Chinese internet names amid rising concerns that they could
be displaced by AI agents over the longer term, leading to a sell off alongside with
the U.S. software stocks;
 Weak inflows as southbound flows slowing down coupled with  elevated capital
raising activities from IPO and secondary offerings;

 Significant earnings downgrade (-37%) in the past 12 months primarily driven
by competition among food delivery platforms with the three companies
(Meituan, JD, Alibaba) made up over 70% of the earnings cut;
 Foreign investors selling out of EM equities post the Iran conflict began which
would have affected HK equities.
Last month we held the tactical view (see here) that HSTECH could perform well given a
myriad of catalysts coming up in the next 2 months. Many of these catalysts have come

through  including:
 Launch of the new LLM models from Deepseek and Tencent;
 Food delivery competition easing in the quarterly results;
 US China leadership summit leading to a potential relaxation of export control on
AI chips;
 EV demand to see some upside as oil price stay higher for longer.
With the market not heeding these positive developments, we now think it may take
longer for HSTECH to outperform. Our recent discussions with investors suggest that

their attention is focused around hardware tech, power equipment and other North
Asian names which may have drawn liquidity away from HK internet names.

Source: FactSet, UBS

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
For various stocks across the industries we cover in the Hong Kong and mainland China stock
markets, we use a variety of valuation approaches, including DCF models, Gordon growth
model analysis and relative valuation analysis using various multiples such as PE, EV/EBITDA
and P/BV.
We think the risks facing China's equities include a hard landing for the property market, a
capital exodus associated with currency depreciation and slow structural reform progress. In

our view, any government policies that do not adequately address these risks could result in a
shock to the market. For example, an excess of stimulus policies could pose a risk to the
transition from an investment-driven to a consumption-driven economy and increase the
debt of government and state-owned enterprises.
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

新應材 4749 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
向「光」而行 
投資與評價 
1. 1Q26 受惠於晶圓廠客戶新廠洗線+海外廠提前拉貨，營收略優於我們
先前預估，EPS 達 3.66 元，優於我們原先預估的 3.09 元。 
2. 展望 2Q26，預估 5 月在海外廠恢復拉貨且 N2 投片量逐漸成長下，營
收將重回月增軌道。整體而言，預估 2Q26 營收季減至 11.94 億元。 
3. 預估新應材 2H26 將增加折舊費用，將影響毛利率表現，研究部認為擴
充產能效益將於 FY27 後逐漸顯現。 
4. 預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 16.01 元/22.91 元，YoY＋42.2%/+43.1%，
獲利成長具長期發展性。研究部維持買進投資建議，目標價 1,310 元，
以 57x FY27 獲利預估。 
重點更新 
雖 FY26 面臨折舊逆風，營運動能將於 FY27 後逐漸發酵 
研究部認為 FY26 主要營收成長動能為大客戶兩座 N2 新廠於今年啟用。為
因應大客戶積極擴廠，新應材須提前擴充產能，2H26 將增加折舊費用，與規
模經濟效益成長相互抵銷，FY26 全年毛利率持穩於 43.6%。展望 FY27，隨
著大客戶海內外各地新廠持續逐步開出，成長性相較 FY26 將更為顯著。 
台廠光阻領域之王持續拓展產品領域 
先進製程方面 ， 研究部預期至A10 製程將有新產品突破 。KrF 光阻劑若 FY27
客戶驗證通過後，將應用於 A10 製程；EBR/BARC 新品驗證通過後，亦將應
用於 A10 製程。此外 ，ArF 光阻劑亦持續研發中。先進封裝方面，Protection 
Layer、高深寬比光阻、封裝膠等多項產品持續研發中，跨足包括 SOIC、
CoWoS、CPO 等多種封裝平台，有望與新寶紘共同開發先進封裝領域材料。 
中長期成長具有想像空間 
至 FY30 前為大客戶快速擴充先進製程與先進封裝產能階段，將直接帶動材
料供應鏈營運動能。若新應材成功跨入光阻劑領域，將為台廠特化廠重要突
破，獨特產業地位為評價帶來支撐。研究部看好產品組合逐漸完善，光阻劑
研發持續進行、開始跨足先進封裝用材料，同時持續與策略夥伴合作持續拓
展更多特化領域並鞏固上游關鍵原料資源，在半導體在地化趨勢下，研究部
認為新應材未來年年皆有新品持續加入產品組合 ， 公司營運成長具想像空間 。 
王筠婷 / 特用化學、半導體週邊 
+886-2-2326-9899#7961 
vanessawang@cathayfut.com.tw

### Page 2

訪談內容 
1Q26 洗線+海外營收貢獻，營收優於我們先前預估 
1Q26 受惠於晶圓廠客戶兩座台灣廠區須開始啟用 ，前置作業需使用大量的特用化學品進行洗線 ，
另因中東戰爭因素 ， 客戶海外廠提前拉貨 ， 皆帶動新應材1Q26 營收動能。營收季增至 12.46 億
元(QoQ+14.9%/YoY+29.9%)，略優於我們先前預估的 11.37 億元。稼動率提升帶動毛利率表
現，季增至 45.4%(QoQ+1.4 ppts/ YoY+6.4 ppts)，略優於我們原先預估的 44.2%，業外表現
亦優於我們原先預期，EPS 達 3.66 元(QoQ+29.8%/ YoY+63.4%)，優於我們原先預估的 3.09
元。 
展望 2Q26，4 月因面臨客戶海外廠提前至 Q1 拉貨，且無客戶新廠洗線收入，使月營收呈現月
減年減僅達 3.5 億元，研究部預估 5 月在海外廠恢復拉貨且 N2 投片量逐漸成長下，營收將重回
月增軌道。整體而言，研究部預估 2Q26 營收季減至 11.94 億元(QoQ-4.1%/YoY+3.6%)，些微
下修營收預估。預估毛利率受稼動率影響，季減至 42.7%(QoQ-2.7 ppts/ YoY-3.6 ppts)，EPS
為 3.34 元(QoQ-4.1%/ YoY+8.8%)。

表一. 1Q26 財報與原先預期對比                                     (單位﹔百萬元) 
新應材 1Q26A 實際值 原先預估 差異幅度 差異分析 
營收 1,246 1,137 +9.6% 
1. 晶圓廠客戶兩座台灣廠區開始啟用，前
置作業需使用特化品進行洗線 
2. 因中東戰爭因素，客戶海外廠提前拉貨 
毛利率 45.4% 44.2% +1.2ppts 稼動率提升帶動毛利率表現 
營業利益率 27.1% 26.8% +0.3ppts  
EPS 3.66 3.09 +18.4% 業外表現優於我們原先預期 
資料來源：新應材，國泰證期研究部整理

雖 FY26 面臨折舊逆風，營運動能將於 FY27 後逐漸發酵 
綜觀 FY26，研究部認為新應材主要營收成長動能為 大客戶兩座 N2 新廠於今年啟用，
Rinse/BARC/EBR 三項產品營收將隨 N2 產 量 逐季 成 長，預估 FY26 營收達 54.5 億元
(YoY+27.9%)。利潤率方面，為因應大客戶至 2030 年前海內外積極擴廠潮，新應材須提前擴充
產能，因此 2H26 將增加折舊費用，研究部預估將影響毛利率表現，與產量擴張帶來之規模經濟
效益相互抵銷，FY26 全年毛利率持穩於 43.6%(YoY+0.5 ppts)。營業費用方面，由於啟動多項
研發專案，研發投入維持較高強度，支應製程開發以及產品驗證需求，且營收基期仍處成長初期
階段，研發費用佔比將持續維持高檔。研究部預估 FY26 全年 EPS 為 16.01 元(YoY+42.2%)。 
展望 FY27，研究部認為擴充產能效益將於 FY27 後逐漸顯現。隨著大客戶海內外各地新廠持續
逐步開出，FY27 後新廠開出規模將高於 FY26，相關半導體材料需求將隨產能擴張同步成長，將
直接挹注 Rinse 獨供商的新應材營運動能，預估 FY27 營收達 74.3 億元(YoY+36.3%)。利潤率
將受惠於規模經濟效益逐漸顯現而提升，預估 FY27 全年毛利率達 44.0%(YoY+0.4 ppts)，全年
EPS 為 22.91 元(YoY+43.1%)，成長性相較 FY26 將更為顯著。

### Page 3

台廠光阻領域之王持續拓展產品領域 
新應材深耕於黃光微影製程領域，銷售之 Rinse、EBR 及 BARC 在客戶端具有獨特供應鏈地位，
目前亦積極拓展新產品領域，於先進製程方面，研究部預期至 A10 製程將有新產品突破。KrF 光
阻劑研發進度符合原先預期，若 FY27 客戶驗證通過後，將應用於A10 製程；EBR/BARC 新品驗
證通過後，亦將應用於 A10 製程。此外，ArF 光阻劑亦持續研發中。 
先進封裝材料領域方面 ，Protection Layer、 高深寬比光阻 、 封裝膠等多項產品持續研發拓展中 ，
跨足領域包括 SOIC、CoWoS、CPO 等多種封裝平台，有望與新寶紘共同開發多項先進封裝領
域材料，綜效效益逐漸顯現。

### Page 4

中長期成長具有想像空間 
至 FY30 前為台系晶圓廠客戶快速擴充先進製程與先進封裝產能階段。今年台灣有兩座 N2 廠開
始啟用，後續至 FY27/FY28 新廠啟用數量也將持續成長，將直接帶動材料供應鏈營運動能，作
為 Rinse 獨供商之新應材將優先受惠。綜觀公司營運表現，研究部預估 FY26/27 整體營收分別
為 54.5/74.3 億元(YoY+27.9%/+36.3%)，毛利率分別為 43.6%/44.0%。 
從評價面來看，若新應材成功跨入光阻劑領域，將為台廠特化廠重要突破，獨特產業地位為其評
價帶來支撐。中長期而言，研究部看好新應材產品組合逐漸完善，光阻劑研發持續進行、開始跨
足先進封裝用材料，同時持續與策略夥伴合作（新寶紘、富宸材料、昱鐳、歐利得）持續拓展更
多特化材料領域並鞏固上游關鍵原料資源，在半導體在地化趨勢下，研究部認為新應材未來年年
皆有新品持續加入產品組合，公司 營運成長 具想像空間 。新應材目前交易於 68/48 倍之
2026/2027 年獲利預估 ， 過去歷史本益比區間為50X~75X， 預估FY26/27 EPS 分別為 16.01 元
/22.91 元，YoY＋42.2%/+43.1%，獲利成長具長期發展性。研究部維持買進投資建議，目標價
1,310 元，以 57x FY27 獲利預估。

產業概況 
光阻周邊市場份額集中度高，台廠迎來新契機 
隨製程越演先進，在半導體微影製程中除了光阻劑本身外，另有一系列光阻周邊材料扮演關鍵角
色，包括表面改質劑 （Rinse） 、洗邊劑 （EBR） 、底部抗反射層 （BARC） 、頂部抗反射層 （TARC）、
稀釋劑 （Thinner） 以及光阻剝除劑 （Stripper） 等。根據研調機構Deloitte 及 WSTS 資料顯示，
2025 年全球黃光微影材料中，光阻及光阻周邊市場規模達67 億美元，預估至2030 年市場規模
將進一步成長至 96 億美元。除光阻劑市場規模增長，製程節點逐漸縮小，先進製程的高良率要
求亦推升對 Rinse、BARC 等提升良率材料的用量 ， 對於光阻周邊材料的材料精密度亦有所提升 。
光阻周邊材料市場集中度高 ， 主要由少數日 、 美 、 德大廠壟斷主要份額 ， 國外廠商如美商Merck、
美商杜邦、日商東京應化等。但在半導體在地化趨勢下，亦為台廠帶來新契機，相關廠商如新應
材(4749 TT)。

圖 3. 光阻領域各產品之供應商

資料來源：新應材，國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 5

公司簡介 
新應材為台廠唯一光阻周邊產品供應商 
新應材股份有限公司成立於 2003 年，由面板光阻材料及清洗劑起家，自 2018 年開始轉型半導
體材料開發，聚焦對良率至關重要的微影（Lithography）特化材料，為台灣唯一在半導體微影
製程從原料合成、純化到配方自主設計，並持續擴大量產的特化材料廠。 
目前業務可分為 1) 半導體特化材料 ： 產品包括微影製程所需的表面改質劑(Rinse)、 洗邊劑(EBR)、 
清洗劑(Cleaner)、 顯影劑(Developer)、 底部抗反射層(BARC)等 ， 另有先進封裝材料及光學元件 。
2) 顯示器特化材料：產品包括顯示器用光阻劑、高/低折射率材料等。2024 年半導體特化材料
營收占比約八成，顯示器特化材料約兩成。 
生產基地方面，公司研發總部位於桃園龍潭，並在台南及高雄設有生產基地。台南廠為配方廠，
高雄一期則為合成及純化製程，高雄二期則涵蓋前段合成及純化以及後段的配方製程。

ESG 政策 
新應材由於 2024 年尚未達 20 億台幣資本額，目前尚未受到強制規範出具 ESG 報告書，預計將
於 2025 年 8 月開始出具 ESG 報告書。

⚫ 環境安全政策 
因產品、活動、服務過程中對人員健康安全危害及所產生的污染與能、資源之耗用對環境產生了
衝擊，為善盡社會之責任，我們將致力於危害、污染預防及持續改善，以降低各項製程及產品對
環境的危害，新應材公司已取得 ISO 45001 職安衛管理系統與 ISO 14001 環境管理系統認證，
定期執行內外部稽核，持續改善。

⚫ 節能減排政策 
新應材制定聚醯亞胺(PI) 溶液回收計劃。分離廢液中的 PI 及溶劑並同時再生及重覆使用，朝向
零液體排放前進 ， 大量減少焚燒處理後產生大量溫室氣體對環境的衝擊問題以及減少石化資源無
法再生循環使用的問題。新應材已榮獲重要晶圓代工客戶頒發「供應商節能減碳輔導專案」感謝
狀。

⚫ 綠色產品政策 
新應材為確保公司在全球供應鏈中不支持或助長任何形式的武裝衝突和人權侵犯 ， 並促進負責任
的採購行為。新應材承諾不使用並要求所有相關供應商配合禁用「來自衝突區域的金屬礦產」 。
新應材將對所有相關供應商進行盡職調查，確保其遵守新應材的政策。對於揭露資訊不完整或是
來自非合格熔煉廠者，新應材將持續督促其改善，並且不排除尋找其他供應商作為替代方案。

EPS 20.17 元 ， 優於元富預估的18.69 元 ， 獲利達成率109%。1Q26
營收 490.38 億元，+2.6%QOQ，+110.2%YOY，AI 續強。1Q26 毛
利率 29.8%，優於元富預估的 26.8%，AI 相關產品比重提升，本業
獲利達成率 114%，業外損失影響，1Q26 EPS 20.17 元。 
 營運逐季成長，GPU 及 ASIC 兩塊業務均樂觀:2026 全年度樂觀看
待，營收逐季成長，故元富維持 2Q26 獲利預估數字。Vera Rubin
世代產品近期小批量生產，Vera Rubin 採 Fanless 設計，其他零部
件相關的水冷應用如 CX9 的網卡、配電板、收發模組、交換機都會
使用水冷產品，同時也新增了 Rack Manifold 的產能，將為 Vera 
Rubin 世代新成長貢獻，公司為 First design partner。關於近期市場
討論水冷板不鍍金事件 ， 公司表示目前正在做最終確認， 因原本鍍金
前期就是外包，故外包取消對奇鋐營運亦無影響。 
 維持買進評等，目標價 3,300 元：1Q26 毛利率優於預期，ASIC 及
GPU 陣營雙動能，水冷擴張趨勢明確，故維持買進，預估 2026 年
EPS 96.56 元，預估 2027 年 EPS 132 元，目標價 3,300 元。

營運逐季成長，GPU 及 ASIC 兩塊業務均樂觀 
近期市場對於 2Q26 營收下修至持平甚至衰退抱持疑慮 ， 公司表示2026 全年度
樂觀看待， 營收逐季成長 ，獲利亦逐季成長， 故元富維持2Q26 獲利預估數字，
預估 2Q26 營收 500.19 億元，QOQ+2%，YOY+69%，毛利率 30.2%，預估
2Q26 EPS 22.14 元。Vera Rubin 世代產品近期小批量生產，Vera Rubin 採
Fanless 設計， 其他零部件相關的水冷應用如CX9 的網卡 、 配電板 、收發模組、
交換機都會使用水冷產品，同時也新增了 Rack Manifold 的產能，將為 Vera 
Rubin 世代新成長貢獻，公司為 First design partner。關於近期市場討論水冷板
不鍍金事件，公司表示目前正在做最終確認，因原本鍍金前期就是外包，故外
包取消對奇鋐營運亦無影響 。LPU 及 CPO 相關業務持續在設計開發 ， 量產時程
取決於客戶，未來的 AI 晶片水冷擴張趨勢明確，ASIC 亦為奇鋐 2027 年重要成
長動能，長線成長性樂觀看待。

維持買進，目標價 3,300 元

維持買進評等，目標價 3,300 元 
1Q26 毛利率優於預期，ASIC 及 GPU 陣營雙動能，水冷擴張趨勢明確，故維
持買進，預估 2026 年 EPS 96.56 元(原估 80.28 元)，預估 2027 年 EPS 132
元，目標價 3,300 元。(2027 PER 25x)

風險提示 
出貨不如預期，ASP 不如預期。

## Document 360: 凱基投顧_ADI Analog Device_陳仲瑋_20260521

### Page 1

五月 21, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  半導體 ‧美國

Analog Device 
(ADI.O/ADI US)

2Q26 財報亮眼，營收創高及展望佳 
重要訊息  
ADI 公告 2Q26 財報與展望均優於市場預期。  
評論及分析  
2Q26 財報優於市場預期 。營收 US$36.23 億，季增 14.7%，超越上季指引
的 US$34-36 億，並優於市場共識的 US$35.02 億，除了工業產品略低於市
場預期外，其餘消費性、通訊 、車用產品均優於市場預期，且資料中心營
收已佔通訊業務的 75%以上。毛利率 73%、 營益率49%、 每股盈餘US$3.09
均優於市場預期的 72.2%/47.9%/US$2.92。  
產能利用率近乎 滿載。公司預期 3Q26 營收 US$38-40 億，按中值計算季增
7.6%，優於市場共識的 US$36.3 億。工業與車用預估維持 季增高個位數 百
分比、通訊產品季增 10-15%、消費性則季減個位數 百分比。毛利率受一次
性收益影響預估下滑 0.5%至 72.5%，營益率預估 48-50%，每股盈餘
US$3.15 -3.45，均優於市場預期 的 71.6%/48%/US$3.03 元。鑒於產能利用
率已接近滿載，毛利率提升的貢獻將主要來自於產品組合改善或外包產能
的增加。 
持續看好營收與獲利率增長空間 。公司擬以 US$15 億收購 Empower 
Semiconductor 布局資料中心的電源管理 IC，儘管該收購案對比公司年營
收(約 US$0.4 億)溢價過高，但我們預期 800V 架構對功率半導體內涵價值
具 10 倍成長空間， 公司有望維持優於同業的營收增速，並將在 2027 年開
始貢獻營收。 同時，市場擔憂的短期毛利率見頂，忽略了公司仍有望透過
漲價以反映市場需求，並持續擴大內部與外包產能。  
投資建議  
我們維持「增加持股」評等， 反映優於預期的財報與展望， 上調 2026-28
年每股盈餘至 US$12.4/$15.2/$17.6 ，並將目標價上調至 US$440 元，以
2027 年每股盈餘及正一倍本益比 29 倍得出。 
投資風險  
關稅壁壘、工業與車用需求持續下滑、競爭者市占率提升。

### Page 5

美國 Analog Devices

五月 21, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  5

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用
事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例 
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物 
總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
環保支出 環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出 
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減
或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作 
每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數 
股東治理分數 衡量公司用以反收購工具的有效性 
公司治理分數 衡量公司對最佳治理原則的承諾和有效性 
產品責任分數 衡量公司生產優質產品和服務的能力，且產品是否將客戶的健康、安全、整合性和數據隱私進行綜合考量 
社區關係分數 衡量公司對成為優良公民、保護公眾健康和尊重商業道德的承諾 
勞動力分數 衡量公司在員工工作滿意度、健康、工作場所的安全、多樣性、平等以及員工發展機會方面的成效 
資源使用指標 衡量公司在原物料、能源或水的使用效率，以及是否通過改進供應鏈來尋求更俱生態效率的解決方案
